function normalizeHeaderText(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/\r?\n/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

function detectHeaderRowIndex(rows) {
  const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
  const scanLimit = Math.min(source.length, 12);
  let bestIndex = -1;
  let bestScore = -Infinity;

  for (let index = 0; index < scanLimit; index += 1) {
    const cells = (source[index] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
    const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
    if (nonEmpty.length < 2) continue;

    const uniqueCount = new Set(nonEmpty).size;
    const duplicatePenalty = nonEmpty.length - uniqueCount;
    const knownHeaderHits = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
      /^(학생ID|학번|성명|사용성명|학년|반|번호|일자|운영일|일정ID|프로그램ID|강좌ID|기록ID|상태|구분|설정키|파일구분|스프레드시트ID|탭명|탭용도)$/.test(value),
    ).length;
    const titlePenalty = nonEmpty.length <= 2 && /UEP|표준|시트|DB|관리|기준/.test(nonEmpty.join(" ")) ? 8 : 0;
    const score = nonEmpty.length * 3 + uniqueCount + knownHeaderHits * 8 - duplicatePenalty * 2 - titlePenalty;

    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestIndex = index;
    }
  }

  return bestIndex;
}

function headerLookupKey(value) {
  return normalizeHeaderText(value).replace(/[\s·_\-()\[\]{}]/g, "").toLowerCase();
}

function valueByHeader(row, ...aliases) {
  if (!row || typeof row !== "object") return "";
  for (const alias of aliases) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(row, alias)) return row[alias];
  }
  const wanted = new Set(aliases.map(headerLookupKey));
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(row)) {
    const normalizedKey = headerLookupKey(key);
    if (wanted.has(normalizedKey)) return value;
    // 병합형 2단 헤더(예: "학생정보 학번", "외출정보 외출일자")도
    // 하위 헤더 별칭으로 안전하게 찾습니다.
    for (const aliasKey of wanted) {
      if (aliasKey && normalizedKey.endsWith(aliasKey)) return value;
    }
  }
  return "";
}

function looksLikeSubHeaderRow(row) {
  const cells = (Array.isArray(row) ? row : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
  const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
  if (nonEmpty.length < 2) return false;
  const headerTerms = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
    /^(?:원점수|표준점수|백분위|등급|과목|학생ID|학번|성명|이름|시험명|시험월|시험년도|학년도|반|번호|일자|날짜|외출일자|외출시간|복귀예정시간|귀사예정시간|자택도착예정시간|사유|목적지|구분|상태|처리상태|승인자|비고)$/.test(value),
  ).length;
  const numericLike = nonEmpty.filter((value) => /^[-+]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$/.test(value)).length;
  return headerTerms >= 2 || (headerTerms >= 1 && numericLike === 0);
}

function composeTwoLevelHeaders(parentRow, childRow) {
  const parents = (Array.isArray(parentRow) ? parentRow : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
  const children = (Array.isArray(childRow) ? childRow : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
  const width = Math.max(parents.length, children.length);
  const result = [];
  let activeParent = "";
  for (let index = 0; index < width; index += 1) {
    const parent = parents[index] || "";
    const child = children[index] || "";
    if (parent) activeParent = parent;
    if (child) {
      const parentKey = headerLookupKey(activeParent);
      const childKey = headerLookupKey(child);
      result.push(parentKey && parentKey !== childKey ? `${activeParent} ${child}`.trim() : child);
    } else {
      result.push(parent || activeParent || `__EMPTY_${index + 1}`);
    }
  }
  return result;
}

ipcMain.handle("notice:delete", async (_event, id = "") => {
  try {
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath()); if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account), matrix=await readNoticeSheetMatrix(token,"40_공지마감","A1:V2000");
    const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["공지ID","상태"]); if(!header) throw new Error("40_공지마감 헤더를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    const h=header.index, headers=header.headers, idCol=headers.indexOf("공지ID"), statusCol=headers.indexOf("상태"), modifiedCol=headers.indexOf("수정일시");
    const rowIndex=matrix.findIndex((r,i)=>i>h&&String(r?.[idCol]||"")===String(id)); if(rowIndex<0) throw new Error("삭제할 공지를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    if(statusCol>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_공지마감'!${columnLetter(statusCol)}${rowIndex+1}:${columnLetter(statusCol)}${rowIndex+1}` ,[["삭제"]]);
    if(modifiedCol>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_공지마감'!${columnLetter(modifiedCol)}${rowIndex+1}:${columnLetter(modifiedCol)}${rowIndex+1}` ,[[new Date().toISOString()]]);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,id};
  } catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||"공지 삭제 실패"};}
});
ipcMain.handle("notice:receiptSave", async (_event, payload={}) => {
  try {
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath()); if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account), now=new Date().toISOString();
    const noticeId=String(payload.noticeId||"").trim(), teacher=String(payload.teacher||"").trim(), userId=String(payload.userId||"").trim();
    if(!noticeId||!teacher) throw new Error("공지ID와 교사명이 필요합니다.");
    const matrix=await readNoticeSheetMatrix(token,"44_공지확인현황","A1:Q3000");
    const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["기록ID","공지ID","교사명","확인상태","제출상태"]); if(!header) throw new Error("44_공지확인현황 헤더를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    const h=header.index, headers=header.headers, idx=name=>headers.indexOf(name);
    const rowIndex=matrix.findIndex((r,i)=>i>h&&String(r?.[idx("공지ID")]||"")===noticeId&&((userId&&String(r?.[idx("사용자ID")]||"")===userId)||String(r?.[idx("교사명")]||"")===teacher));
    const current=rowIndex>=0?matrix[rowIndex]:[];
    const confirmed=payload.confirmed!=null?Boolean(payload.confirmed):String(current?.[idx("확인상태")]||"N").toUpperCase()==="Y";
    const submitted=payload.submitted!=null?Boolean(payload.submitted):String(current?.[idx("제출상태")]||"N").toUpperCase()==="Y";
    const read=payload.read!=null?Boolean(payload.read):String(current?.[idx("읽음상태")]||"N").toUpperCase()==="Y";
    const dismissed=payload.dismissed!=null?Boolean(payload.dismissed):String(current?.[idx("공지해제")]||"N").toUpperCase()==="Y";
    const detailViewed=payload.detailViewed!=null?Boolean(payload.detailViewed):String(current?.[idx("상세열람")]||"N").toUpperCase()==="Y";
    const sourceType=String(payload.sourceType||current?.[idx("원본유형")]||"직접공지");
    const recordId=String(current?.[idx("기록ID")]||`NACK-${crypto.randomUUID()}`);
    const confirmedAt=confirmed?(String(current?.[idx("확인일시")]||"")||now):"";
    const submittedAt=submitted?(String(current?.[idx("제출일시")]||"")||now):"";
    const row=[recordId,noticeId,userId,teacher,String(payload.grade||""),String(payload.classNo||""),confirmed?"Y":"N",confirmedAt,submitted?"Y":"N",submittedAt,now,teacher,String(payload.note||"UEP 상태 처리"),sourceType,read?"Y":"N",dismissed?"Y":"N",detailViewed?"Y":"N"];
    if(rowIndex>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'44_공지확인현황'!A${rowIndex+1}:Q${rowIndex+1}`,[row]);
    else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'44_공지확인현황'!A:Q",[row]);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,recordId,confirmed,submitted,read,dismissed,detailViewed};
  } catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||"공지 확인상태 저장 실패"};}
});

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
}

// __UEP_GOOGLE_MISSING_CREDENTIAL_08069__
async function readEncrypted(filePath) {
  let encrypted = null;
  try { encrypted = await fs.readFile(filePath); } catch (readError) {
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
      try { return await readCredentialRecovery(); } catch {}
    }
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath()) && (readError?.code === "ENOENT" || /no such file/i.test(String(readError?.message||"")))) {
      const friendly = new Error("담임 배포 PC의 Google 시트 연결 승인이 아직 준비되지 않았습니다.");
      friendly.code = "UEP_GOOGLE_CREDENTIAL_MISSING";
      friendly.setupRequired = true;
      throw friendly;
    }
    throw readError;
  }
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    try { return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted)); } catch {}
  }
  // 일부 구버전/수동 이관 파일이 평문 JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.
  try {
    const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
    if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, legacy); return legacy; }
  } catch {}
  // Google 서비스 계정은 safeStorage가 깨져도 동일 PC/사용자용 AES-GCM 복구본으로 자동 복원합니다.
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
    try {
      const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
      if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
        try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
        return recovered;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
  try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
  const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
  error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
  throw error;
}

const UEP_SCHOOL_READ_API_FALLBACK='https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbyLEEam4L2sX653pii69TC3_84wTyfAed_UL8HRY6PBG467im1LmZJ0CIpSKSOj4r6-0w/exec';
let schoolReadPolicyCache={url:'',expiresAt:0};

async function loadSchoolReadApiUrl(){
  if(schoolReadPolicyCache.url&&schoolReadPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now())return schoolReadPolicyCache.url;

async function getOrCreateSchoolReadDeviceId(){

}

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}

}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}


function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}
function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}

    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}


function columnLetter(index) {
  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}
function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}
async function saveStudentContact(payload={}) {
  const allowed={phone:["학생연락처"],email:["학생이메일","이메일","이메일주소"],guardian1Phone:["보호자1연락처"],guardian2Phone:["보호자2연락처"]};
  const field=String(payload.field||"");
  if(!allowed[field]) throw new Error("수정할 수 없는 학생정보 항목입니다.");
  const role=String(payload.role||"");
  if(!["admin","homeroom"].includes(role)) throw new Error("학생 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
  if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
  if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
  const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
  let fieldCol=-1;
  for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
  if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
  let dataRow=-1;
  for(let r=header.index+1;r<matrix.length;r+=1){ if(String(matrix[r]?.[idCol]||"").trim()===studentId){dataRow=r;break;} }
  const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
  if(dataRow<0){
    const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
    row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
    const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);
  }else{
    const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
    const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);
    await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'04_학생연락식별정보'!${col}${sheetRow}:${col}${sheetRow}`,[[value]]);
  }
  liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
  return {ok:true,data};
}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

async function findNeisSchool({ schoolName, officeCode = "", schoolCode = "", apiKey = "" }) {
  if (officeCode && schoolCode) return { schoolName, officeCode, schoolCode };
  const payload = await fetchJson(neisUrl("schoolInfo", { SCHUL_NM: schoolName }, apiKey));
  const rows = neisRows(payload, "schoolInfo");
  const exact = rows.find((row) => String(row.SCHUL_NM || "").replace(/\s/g, "") === String(schoolName || "").replace(/\s/g, ""));
  const row = exact || rows[0];
  if (!row) throw new Error(neisResultMessage(payload, "schoolInfo") || `${schoolName} 학교정보를 찾지 못했습니다.`);
  return {
    schoolName: row.SCHUL_NM || schoolName,
    officeCode: row.ATPT_OFCDC_SC_CODE,
    schoolCode: row.SD_SCHUL_CODE,
    officeName: row.ATPT_OFCDC_SC_NM || "",
    address: row.ORG_RDNMA || row.ORG_RDNZC || "",
  };
}

function cleanMealText(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/<br\s*\/?\s*>/gi, "\n")
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, (match) => match)
    .split(/\n+/)
    .map((item) => item.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

async function fetchNeisMeals(school, targetDate, apiKey = "") {
  const payload = await fetchJson(neisUrl("mealServiceDietInfo", {
    ATPT_OFCDC_SC_CODE: school.officeCode,
    SD_SCHUL_CODE: school.schoolCode,
    MLSV_YMD: ymd(targetDate),
  }, apiKey));
  const rows = neisRows(payload, "mealServiceDietInfo");
  return rows.map((row) => ({
    date: row.MLSV_YMD || ymd(targetDate),
    mealType: row.MMEAL_SC_NM || "급식",
    menu: cleanMealText(row.DDISH_NM),
    origin: cleanMealText(row.ORPLC_INFO),
    calories: row.CAL_INFO || "",
    nutrition: cleanMealText(row.NTR_INFO),
  }));
}

function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
  const text = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (!/^\d{8}$/.test(text)) return null;
  const date = new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)), Number(text.slice(4,6))-1, Number(text.slice(6,8)), 12, 0, 0);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
}

    const selected = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, {
      title: "Google 서비스 계정 JSON 선택",
      properties: ["openFile"],
      filters: [{ name: "Google 서비스 계정", extensions: ["json"] }],
    });
    if (selected.canceled || !selected.filePaths[0]) return { ok: false, canceled: true };
    try {
      const credentials = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(selected.filePaths[0], "utf8"));
      if (!validateServiceAccount(credentials)) return { ok: false, reason: "Google 서비스 계정 JSON 형식이 아닙니다." };
      const data = await fetchLiveData({ force: true, credentials });
      await saveEncrypted(credentialPath(), credentials);
      return {
        ok: true,
        account: credentials.client_email,
        sourceName: UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,
        preview: data.summary,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      liveDataCache = null;
      liveDataFetchedAt = 0;
      return { ok: false, reason: `${error.message || "구글시트 연결 실패"} 서비스 계정 이메일에 UEP 구글시트 보기 권한이 있는지 확인하세요.` };
    }
  });
  ipcMain.handle("google:previewReadonlySync", async () => {
    try {
      const data = await fetchLiveData({ force: true });
      return { ok: true, syncedAt: data.syncedAt, preview: data.summary, nightPreview: data.summary, sourceName: data.sourceName };
    } catch (error) {
      liveDataCache = null;
      liveDataFetchedAt = 0;
      return { ok: false, reason: error.message || "UEP 구글시트 새로고침 실패" };
    }
  });
  ipcMain.handle("google:readReadonlyCache", async () => {
    try { return { ok: true, data: await fetchLiveData() }; }
    catch (error) { return { ok: false, reason: error.message || "UEP 구글시트를 읽지 못했습니다." }; }
  });
  

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}

}

    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];

    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,writes);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,count,batchId};
  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}

      if (!validateServiceAccount(credentials)) return { ok: false, reason: "Google 서비스 계정 JSON 형식이 아닙니다." };
      const data = await fetchLiveData({ force: true, credentials });
      await saveEncrypted(credentialPath(), credentials);
      return {
        ok: true,
        account: credentials.client_email,
        sourceName: UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,
        preview: data.summary,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      liveDataCache = null;
      liveDataFetchedAt = 0;
      return { ok: false, reason: `${error.message || "구글시트 연결 실패"} 서비스 계정 이메일에 UEP 구글시트 보기 권한이 있는지 확인하세요.` };
    }
  });
  ipcMain.handle("google:previewReadonlySync", async () => {
    try {
      const data = await fetchLiveData({ force: true });
      return { ok: true, syncedAt: data.syncedAt, preview: data.summary, nightPreview: data.summary, sourceName: data.sourceName };
    } catch (error) {
      liveDataCache = null;
      liveDataFetchedAt = 0;
      return { ok: false, reason: error.message || "UEP 구글시트 새로고침 실패" };
    }
  });
  ipcMain.handle("google:readReadonlyCache", async () => {
    try { return { ok: true, data: await fetchLiveData() }; }
    catch (error) { return { ok: false, reason: error.message || "UEP 구글시트를 읽지 못했습니다." }; }
  });
  

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}
function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}
async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  try{
    const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
    const batchId=String(payload.batchId||'').trim();if(!batchId)throw new Error('입력 배치를 선택해 주세요.');
    const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
    let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];
    for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(String(rows[i]?.[1]||'')===batchId){const sheetRow=i+4;const current=Array.from({length:24},(_,j)=>rows[i]?.[j]??'');current[3]=storedType;current[4]=payload.nature==='보고서'?'보고서 제출':payload.nature;let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(current[16]||''));}catch{}current[6]=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',meta.submittedAt||'');current[17]='검수완료';current[19]=pid;current[20]=pk;current[23]=note||current[23]||'';writes.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!A${sheetRow}:X${sheetRow}`,values:[current]});count++;}
    await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,writes);
    liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,count,batchId};
  }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
}

async function bulkAutoNoticeGovernance(status){
  if(!currentUserCanManageNotices())return;
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true,includeDismissed:true,includeUnapproved:true})
    .filter(item=>item.days>=-3&&item.days<=7&&autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(item.source,item.id)==='pending');
  if(!rows.length)return toast('검토할 자동공지 후보가 없습니다.');
  const label=status==='approved'?'승인':status==='handled'?'확인':status==='blocked'?'불가(해제)':'처리';
  if(!confirm(`현재 자동공지 후보 ${rows.length}건을 일괄 ${label} 처리할까요?`))return;
  state.autoNoticeGovernance=state.autoNoticeGovernance||{};
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),by=currentLoginTeacherName()||'관리자';
  rows.forEach(item=>{state.autoNoticeGovernance[autoNoticeGovernanceKey(item.source,item.id)]={status,note:`관리자 일괄 ${label}`,by,at:now};});
  await save();render(state.activePage||'dashboard');toast(`자동공지 ${rows.length}건을 ${label} 처리했습니다.`);
}

async function saveAutoNoticeReceipt(source,id,change={}){
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  const classMatch=String(mine||'').match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})(?:반)?$/); const classNo=classMatch?classMatch[1]:'';
  const noticeId=autoNoticeReceiptId(source,id), userId=currentNoticeUserId(), teacher=currentLoginTeacherName()||'사용자';
  const key=`${noticeId}|${userId||teacher}`, previous=currentAutoReceipt(source,id)||{};
  const payload={noticeId,userId,teacher,grade:'1',classNo,sourceType:String(source||'자동공지'),read:true,...change};
  state.autoNoticeReceipts=state.autoNoticeReceipts||{};
  state.autoNoticeReceipts[key]={...previous,...payload,modifiedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  try{await save();}catch{}
  try{
    const result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveNoticeReceipt?.(payload);
    if(!result?.ok){toast(`알림 상태는 이 PC에 반영했습니다. 시트 동기화: ${result?.reason||'저장 실패'}`);return true;}
    try{await refreshReadonlyAfterNotice();}catch{}
    return true;
  }catch(error){toast('알림 상태는 이 PC에 반영했습니다. 시트 연결 시 다시 동기화됩니다.');return true;}
}
function autoAlertAckKey(item){return `${autoAlertOwnerKey()}|${dateKey(today)}|${item.id||item.category||item.title}`;}
function autoAlertIsAcked(item){return Boolean(state.autoAlertAcks?.[autoAlertAckKey(item)]);}

function currentUserCanManageNotices(){return currentRoleId()==="admin"||currentUserIsGradeHead();}
function currentNoticeUserId(){
  return String(state?.auth?.user?.id||state?.auth?.user?.email||state?.currentUser?.id||state?.currentUser?.email||currentLoginTeacherName()||"").trim();
}
function connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser(item){
  if(!item)return false;
  const grade=String(item.targetGrade||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  if(grade&&grade!=="1"&&!currentUserCanManageNotices())return false;
  const target=String(item.targetClass||"전체").trim();
  if(!target||target==="전체"||/전체|1~9|1-9/.test(target))return true;
  if(currentUserCanManageNotices())return true;
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  if(!mine)return true;
  const mineNo=mine.split("-")[1];
  return target.split(/[,·\s]+/).some(v=>String(v).replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")===mineNo||String(v)===mine);
}
function directNoticeRows({activeOnly=true}={}){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const source=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[]);
  // __UEP_08160_NOTICE_LATEST_REVISION__
  const revisionTime=item=>String(item?.modifiedAt||item?.updatedAt||item?.['수정일시']||item?.postDate||item?.date||'');
  const byId=new Map();
  source.forEach((item,index)=>{
    const id=String(item?.id||item?.noticeId||item?.['공지ID']||'').trim()||('__row_'+index);
    const prev=byId.get(id);
    if(!prev||revisionTime(item)>=revisionTime(prev))byId.set(id,item);
  });
  const connected=[...byId.values()].filter(connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser);
  return connected.filter(item=>{
    if(item?.status==="삭제"||item?.status==="종료"||item?.done)return false;
    if(!activeOnly)return true;
    const start=String(item.postDate||item.date||"").slice(0,10), end=String(item.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    if(start&&start>todayKey)return false;
    if(end&&end<todayKey)return false;
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b)=>Number(Boolean(b.important))-Number(Boolean(a.important))||String(b.postDate||b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.postDate||a.date||"")));
}
function currentNoticeReceipt(item){
  const name=currentLoginTeacherName(), userId=currentNoticeUserId();
  return (item?.receipts||[]).find(row=>(userId&&String(row.userId||"")===userId)||String(row.teacher||"").trim()===name)||null;
}
function noticeExpectedCount(item){
  const target=String(item?.targetClass||"전체");
  if(!target||target==="전체"||/전체|1~9|1-9/.test(target))return 9;
  const nums=[...new Set((target.match(/\d+/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(n=>n>=1&&n<=9))];
  return Math.max(1,nums.length||1);
}
function noticeDeadlineTypeLabel(type){
  const value=String(type||'').trim();
  return value==='학생제출'?'학생 제출':value==='담임확인'?'담임 확인':value==='업무처리'?'업무 처리':'마감';
}

async function refreshReadonlyAfterNotice({force=false}={}){
  // 확인/승인 상태는 먼저 로컬 캐시에 즉시 반영한다. 매 클릭마다 전체 시트를 다시 읽지 않는다.

    const sync=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();
    if(sync?.ok&&sync.data){readonlyCache=sync.data;return;}
  }
  const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();
  if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data;
}

function openGradeNotice(id) {
  const notice = state.gradeNotices.find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!notice) return;
  const className = "1-1";
  const checked = notice.confirmedClasses.includes(className);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GRADE NOTICE";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = notice.title;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<div class="program-notice"><b>${escapeHtml(notice.author)} 공지</b><p>${escapeHtml(notice.detail)}</p></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>담임 확인 현황</h4><div class="notice-class-grid">${notice.requiredClasses.map((item) => `<span class="${notice.confirmedClasses.includes(item) ? "done" : ""}"><b>${item}</b><small>${notice.confirmedClasses.includes(item) ? "확인" : "미확인"}</small></span>`).join("")}</div></div><button class="btn ${checked ? "secondary" : "primary"}" id="confirmNoticeButton">${checked ? "확인 취소" : "1-1반 담임 확인"}</button><p class="drawer-note">현재는 로컬 시범 동작입니다. 여러 담임의 실시간 확인 상태는 공용 시트 또는 서버 연결 후 공유됩니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#confirmNoticeButton").onclick = async () => {
    notice.confirmedClasses = checked
      ? notice.confirmedClasses.filter((item) => item !== className)
      : [...notice.confirmedClasses, className];
    await save();
    openGradeNotice(id);
    render("dashboard");
  };
}

function dashboardWorkAlerts(){
  return directNoticeRows().map(item=>{
    const st=noticeActionState(item), needsConfirm=item.confirmRequired&&!st.confirmed, needsSubmit=item.submitRequired&&!st.submitted;
    const priority=item.important?4:item.topAlert?3:item.homeroomNotice?2:(needsConfirm||needsSubmit?1:0);
    return {kind:"notice",item,priority,title:item.title||"학교업무 공지",meta:`${item.noticeKind||item.type||'공지'}${item.homeroomNotice?' · 종례전달':''}${item.deadline?` · ${noticeDeadlineStatus(item)}`:''}${needsConfirm?' · 확인 필요':''}${needsSubmit?' · 제출 필요':''}`};
  }).filter(x=>x.priority>0).sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority).slice(0,8);
}
function dashboardTopAlertsMarkup(){
  const alerts=dashboardWorkAlerts();
  if(!alerts.length)return `<section class="uep-top-alerts is-clear"><button data-open-work-alerts><span>🔔</span><b>새 학교업무 공지 없음</b><small>오늘의 학교업무·공지에서 확인할 내용을 표시합니다.</small><em>확인 ›</em></button></section>`;
  const urgent=alerts[0],noticeCount=alerts.length;
  const level=urgent.priority>=3?"warning":"info";
  return `<section class="uep-top-alerts ${level}"><button data-open-work-alerts><span>🔔</span><b>새 공지 ${noticeCount}</b><small>${escapeHtml(urgent.title)} · ${escapeHtml(urgent.meta)}</small><em>열기 ›</em></button></section>`;
}

function applyStorageLocationBadges(){
  const configs=[["#neisSettingsForm","local","로컬 저장"],["#programSheetForm","local","로컬 저장"],["#updateSettingsForm","local","로컬 저장"],["#programEditor","local","로컬 저장"],["#programOverrideEditor","local","로컬 저장"],["#issueReportForm","local","로컬 저장"],["#studentGuidanceForm","sheet","시트 저장"],["#officialEditorForm","sheet","시트 저장"],["#noticeEditorForm","sheet","시트 저장"]];
  configs.forEach(([selector,kind,label])=>{const root=$(selector);if(!root||root.querySelector('.storage-location-inline'))return;const button=[...root.querySelectorAll('button')].find(b=>/저장|등록|불러오기/.test(b.textContent||''));if(!button)return;const badge=document.createElement('em');badge.className=`storage-badge ${kind} storage-location-inline`;badge.textContent=label;badge.title=kind==='local'?'이 PC에만 저장됩니다':kind==='sheet'?'UEP 표준 Google 시트에 저장됩니다':'중앙 승인 저장소에 저장됩니다';button.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin',badge);});
}
function selectStudentDashboardById(studentId){
  studentSensitiveRevealKeys.clear();
  const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
  const key=String(studentId||'').trim();
  const idx=students.findIndex(student=>String(student.id||'')===key || String(student.studentNo||'')===key);
  if(idx<0)return false;
  studentDashboardIndex=idx;
  selectedStudentId=students[idx].id||students[idx].studentNo||"";
  studentPageMode='dashboard';
  window.__uepStudentDashboardSwitchToken=(window.__uepStudentDashboardSwitchToken||0)+1;
  return true;
}
function switchStudentDashboard(studentId){
  const previous=String(selectedStudentId||'');
  if(!selectStudentDashboardById(studentId))return false;
  const current=(readonlyCache?.students||[])[studentDashboardIndex];
  if(current&&String(current.id||current.studentNo||'')!==previous)touchStudentCardHeat(current);
  render('students');
  window.scrollTo(0,0);
  return true;
}
function uepBindOnce(el,key,event,handler){if(!el)return false;const attr='uepBound'+key;if(el.dataset[attr])return false;el.dataset[attr]='1';el.addEventListener(event,handler);return true;}

// UEP 0.80.79 selection-course identity/comparison helpers.
function uepSelectionStableStudentKey(row={}){
  const sid=String(row.studentId??row.studentID??row['학생ID']??'').trim();
  if(sid) return `ID:${sid}`;
  const sno=String(row.studentNo??row.studentNumber??row['학번']??'').trim();
  return sno?`NO:${sno}`:'';
}
function uepSelectionNormalizeCourse(row={}){
  return String(row.course??row.subject??row['과목']??row['선택과목']??row['과목명']??'').trim();
}
function uepSelectionSemester(row={}){
  return String(row.semester??row['학기']??row['신청학기']??'').trim();
}
function uepSelectionRound(row={}){
  return String(row.round??row['신청차수']??row['신청구분']??'').trim();
}
function uepCompareSelectionHistory(rows=[]){
  const byStudent=new Map();
  for(const row of rows){
    const key=uepSelectionStableStudentKey(row); if(!key) continue;
    if(!byStudent.has(key)) byStudent.set(key,[]);
    byStudent.get(key).push(row);
  }
  const out=[];
  for(const [studentKey,list] of byStudent){
    const semesters=[...new Set(list.map(uepSelectionSemester).filter(Boolean))];
    for(const semester of semesters){
      const semRows=list.filter(r=>uepSelectionSemester(r)===semester);
      const pre=new Set(semRows.filter(r=>/사전|상담|구글폼|google/i.test(uepSelectionRound(r)+' '+String(r['원본시트']??''))).map(uepSelectionNormalizeCourse).filter(Boolean));
      const main=new Set(semRows.filter(r=>/본신청|리로|수강신청/i.test(uepSelectionRound(r)+' '+String(r['원본시트']??''))).map(uepSelectionNormalizeCourse).filter(Boolean));
      if(!pre.size && !main.size) continue;
      const kept=[...pre].filter(x=>main.has(x));
      const removed=[...pre].filter(x=>!main.has(x));
      const added=[...main].filter(x=>!pre.has(x));
      const status=!removed.length&&!added.length?'일치':(removed.length&&added.length?'변경':(added.length?'추가':'삭제'));
      out.push({studentKey,semester,status,kept,removed,added});
    }
  }
  return out;
}

// __UEP_GOOGLE_NONBLOCKING_08080__

(function(){
  function annotateGoogleConnection(){
    const drawer=document.querySelector('#drawerBody');
    if(!drawer) return;
    const text=String(drawer.textContent||'');
    if(!/Google|구글시트/.test(text)) return;
    if(drawer.querySelector('[data-uep-google-nonblocking-note]')) return;
    const note=document.createElement('div');
    note.dataset.uepGoogleNonblockingNote='1';
    note.className='setup-warning';
    note.style.marginTop='10px';
    note.innerHTML='<b>UEP는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.</b><br>Google 연결은 최신 학교자료를 동기화하기 위한 연결입니다. 연결이 끊겨도 마지막 정상 동기화 자료로 화면을 사용할 수 있으며, 연결이 복구되면 최신 자료로 자동 갱신됩니다.';
    drawer.appendChild(note);
  }
  const obs=new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(annotateGoogleConnection));
  obs.observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  setTimeout(annotateGoogleConnection,350);
})();

function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

async function loadNeisDashboard(force = false, requestedDate = "") {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.loadNeisDashboard) {
    neisError = "이 실행환경에서는 NEIS 연결 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return;
  }
  if (neisLoading) return;
  neisLoading = true;
  if (state.activePage === "dashboard" || state.activePage === "timetable" || state.activePage === "settings") render(state.activePage);
  const options = {
    schoolName: state.settings.neis.schoolName,
    officeCode: state.settings.neis.officeCode,
    schoolCode: state.settings.neis.schoolCode,
    grade: state.settings.neis.grade,
    classNo: state.settings.neis.classNo,
    date: requestedDate || dateKey(new Date()),
    useApiKey: Boolean(state.settings.neis.useApiKey),
    comciganSchoolCode: state.settings.neis.comciganSchoolCode || "69092",
    force,
  };
  try {
    const result = await window.schoolBoard.loadNeisDashboard(options);
    if (!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason || "NEIS 자료를 불러오지 못했습니다.");
    neisData = result;
    neisError = result.offline ? result.staleReason || "실시간 연결 실패로 마지막 저장자료를 표시합니다." : "";
    if (result.school) {
      state.settings.neis.schoolName = result.school.schoolName || state.settings.neis.schoolName;
      state.settings.neis.officeCode = result.school.officeCode || state.settings.neis.officeCode;
      state.settings.neis.schoolCode = result.school.schoolCode || state.settings.neis.schoolCode;
      await save();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    neisError = error?.message || String(error);
    if (!neisData?.ok) neisData = null;
  } finally {
    neisLoading = false;
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if (["dashboard", "timetable", "settings"].includes(state.activePage)) render(state.activePage);
  }
}

function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

async function loadNeisDashboard(force = false, requestedDate = "") {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.loadNeisDashboard) {
    neisError = "이 실행환경에서는 NEIS 연결 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return;
  }
  if (neisLoading) return;
  neisLoading = true;
  if (state.activePage === "dashboard" || state.activePage === "timetable" || state.activePage === "settings") render(state.activePage);
  const options = {
    schoolName: state.settings.neis.schoolName,
    officeCode: state.settings.neis.officeCode,
    schoolCode: state.settings.neis.schoolCode,
    grade: state.settings.neis.grade,
    classNo: state.settings.neis.classNo,
    date: requestedDate || dateKey(new Date()),
    useApiKey: Boolean(state.settings.neis.useApiKey),
    comciganSchoolCode: state.settings.neis.comciganSchoolCode || "69092",
    force,
  };
  try {
    const result = await window.schoolBoard.loadNeisDashboard(options);
    if (!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason || "NEIS 자료를 불러오지 못했습니다.");
    neisData = result;
    neisError = result.offline ? result.staleReason || "실시간 연결 실패로 마지막 저장자료를 표시합니다." : "";
    if (result.school) {
      state.settings.neis.schoolName = result.school.schoolName || state.settings.neis.schoolName;
      state.settings.neis.officeCode = result.school.officeCode || state.settings.neis.officeCode;
      state.settings.neis.schoolCode = result.school.schoolCode || state.settings.neis.schoolCode;
      await save();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    neisError = error?.message || String(error);
    if (!neisData?.ok) neisData = null;
  } finally {
    neisLoading = false;
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if (["dashboard", "timetable", "settings"].includes(state.activePage)) render(state.activePage);
  }
}

function toggleDashboardEditing() {
  state.settings.layoutLocked = !state.settings.layoutLocked;
  save();
  render("dashboard");
  toast(state.settings.layoutLocked ? "대시보드 배치를 저장하고 잠갔습니다." : "위젯을 끌어서 순서를 바꾸고 크기를 조절하세요.");
}

function accessControlInlineMarkup(){
  const p=currentUserProfile(), isAdmin=currentRoleId()==="admin";
  const accounts=[...(readonlyCache?.userAccounts||[])].sort((a,b)=>String(a.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.name||''),'ko'));
  const activeCount=accounts.filter(a=>a.active!==false).length, blockedCount=accounts.length-activeCount;
  const roleOptions=["관리자","학년부장","학년총무","담임교사","교과담당","교감","교장"];
  const rows=accounts.length?accounts.map(a=>`<tr data-inline-access-user="${escapeHtml(a.id)}"><td><b>${escapeHtml(a.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(maskEmail(a.email))}</small></td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-role>${roleOptions.map(r=>`<option ${accessAccountRole(a)===r?'selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.admin==='Y'?'관리자':a.role||'교과담당')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-grade>${[1,2,3].map(g=>`<option ${String(a.grade)==String(g)?'selected':''}>${g}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.grade||'-')}</td><td>${isAdmin?`<select data-inline-access-class><option value="">-</option>${Array.from({length:14},(_,i)=>i+1).map(c=>`<option value="${c}" ${String(a.classNo)==String(c)?'selected':''}>${c}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:escapeHtml(a.classNo||'-')}</td><td><span class="state ${a.active!==false?'connected':'error'}">${a.active!==false?'승인':'차단'}</span></td><td>${isAdmin?`<button class="btn secondary" data-inline-access-toggle>${a.active!==false?'사용 중지':'사용 승인'}</button><button class="btn primary" data-inline-access-save>권한 저장</button>`:'-'}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="6">01_사용자계정에 등록된 사용자가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
  return `<article class="setting-card access-control-inline"><div class="connection-title"><div><small>UEP RELEASE SECURITY</small><h3>사용자·권한 승인</h3><p>01_사용자계정의 사용여부·역할·담당학년·담임반을 이 화면에서 바로 관리합니다.</p></div><span class="state connected">계정표 승인</span></div><div class="security-control-grid release-security-grid"><article><small>등록 사용자</small><b>${accounts.length}명</b><p>01_사용자계정 기준</p></article><article><small>사용 승인</small><b>${activeCount}명</b><p>로그인 가능</p></article><article><small>사용 중지</small><b>${blockedCount}명</b><p>로그인 차단</p></article><article><small>현재 관리자</small><b>${escapeHtml(p.name||'-')}</b><p>${escapeHtml(currentRoleDisplay())}</p></article></div><div class="access-user-table-wrap"><table class="access-user-table"><thead><tr><th>사용자</th><th>역할</th><th>학년</th><th>담임반</th><th>상태</th><th>관리</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div><div class="connection-actions"><button class="btn secondary" data-inline-access-refresh>계정표 새로고침</button></div><p class="safe-note">현재 로그인한 관리자 자기 계정은 사용 중지할 수 없습니다.</p></article>`;

const UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY='uep_subject_confidential_unlocked_v3';
function subjectConfidentialAllowed(){
  const role=String(currentRoleId?.()||'');
  return ['admin','grade_head'].includes(

onfidentialPasswordConfigured();
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured});
  if(!result)return false;
  if(configured&&await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.current)!==subjectConfidentialPasswordHash()){
    toast('현재 선택과목 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');return false;

function lockSubjectConfidential(){sessionStorage.removeItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY);}
async function unlockSubjectConfidential(){
  // __UEP_08159_HOMEROOM_SUBJECT_GATE__
  const scope=typeof currentTeacherAccessScope==='function'?currentTeacherAccessScope():null;
  const role=String(currentRoleId?.()||'');
  const homeroomAllowed=role==='homeroom'||Boolean(scope?.teacher&&scope?.classNo);
  if(!subjectConfidentialAllowed()&&!homeroomAllowed){toast('담임·학년부장·관리자는 비밀번호 인증 후 과목별 신청현황을 열람할 수 있습니다.');return false;}
  if(sessionStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY)==='1')return true;
  if(!subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured()){toast('설정 → 사용자·보안에서 선택과목 대외비 비밀번호를 먼저 설정해 주세요.');return false;}
  const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'unlock',configured:true});
  if(!result)return false;
  if(await subjectConfidentialDigest(result.first)!==subjectConfidentialPasswordHash()){toast('선택과목 대외비 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');return false;}
  sessionStorage.setItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_SESSION_KEY,'1');
  return true;
}

function classNumberOf(student) {
  return String(student?.className || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
}
function availableClasses() {
  const found = [...new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).map(classNumberOf).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  return found.length ? found : Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1));
}
function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");
}

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}
function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}

function availableClasses() {
  const found = [...new Set((readonlyCache?.students || []).map(classNumberOf).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));

function optionStudents(classNo, selectedId = "") {
  return (readonlyCache?.students || []).filter(s => !classNo || classNumberOf(s) === String(classNo)).map(s => `<option value="${escapeHtml(s.id)}" ${s.id === selectedId ? "selected" : ""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(s.name)}</option>`).join("");

function scorePeriodKey(row) {
  const year = String(row?.schoolYear || today.getFullYear()).replace(/[^0-9]/g, "") || String(today.getFullYear());
  let semester = String(row?.semester || "").match(/[12]/)?.[0] || "";
  if (!semester && row?.scoreType === "모의고사") {
    const month = Number(String(row.exam || "").match(/(\d{1,2})\s*월/)?.[1] || 0);
    semester = month && month >= 8 ? "2" : "1";
  }
  return `${year}-${semester || "1"}`;
}
function scorePeriodLabel(key) {
  if (!key || key === "all") return "전체 누적";
  const [year, semester] = String(key).split("-");
  return `${year}학년도 ${semester || "1"}학기`;
}
function availableScorePeriods(mode = scoreMode) {
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.scoreRecords || []).filter(row => mode === "combined" || row.scoreType === (mode === "mock" ? "모의고사" : "내신"));
  return [...new Set(rows.map(scorePeriodKey).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
}
function scoreExamOrder(exam) {
  const order = ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사","3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사","수능"];
  const index = order.indexOf(exam);
  return index < 0 ? 999 : index;
}
function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
  const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
  const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
  if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
  const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
  if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
  const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
  if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
  return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
}
function gradeToneClass(value) {
  const grade = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(grade) && grade >= 1 && grade <= 9 ? `grade-${grade}` : "grade-na";
}
function gradeBadgeMarkup(value, suffix = "등급") {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined || value === "-") return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">-</span>`;
  const number = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(number)) return `<span class="grade-badge grade-na">${escapeHtml(String(value))}</span>`;
  return `<span class="grade-badge ${gradeToneClass(number)}"><b>${scoreDisplay(number,2)}</b><small>${suffix}</small></span>`;
}

function masterTimetableRows({grade=1,classNo="",teacher=""}={}){
  // 배포판에서는 연결 전 샘플/고정 시간표를 실제 자료처럼 표시하지 않는다.
  // 시간표마스터가 구글시트에서 정상 수신된 경우에만 렌더링한다.
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.timetableRows)?readonlyCache.timetableRows:[];
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    if(String(row["학년도"]||"")!=="2026"||String(row["학기"]||"")!=="2")return false;
    if(grade&&String(row["학년"]||"")!==String(grade))return false;
    if(classNo&&String(row["반"]||"")!==String(classNo))return false;
    if(teacher&&uep08123TeacherKey(row["교사명"]||row["교사"]||row.teacher||"")!==uep08123TeacherKey(teacher))return false;
    return String(row["적용상태"]||"확정").trim()!=="취소";
  });
}
function teacherTimetableMasterRows(teacher=""){
  const rows=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.teacherTimetableRows)?readonlyCache.teacherTimetableRows:[];
  return rows.filter(row=>{
    if(String(row["학년도"]||"")!=="2026"||String(row["학기"]||"")!=="2")return false;
    if(teacher&&uep08123TeacherKey(row["교사명"]||"")!==uep08123TeacherKey(teacher))return false;
    return String(row["적용상태"]||"확정").trim()!=="취소";
  });
}
function masterTeacherTimetableData(teacher){
  // 0.81.45: 학급시간표가 아니라 교사별 PDF 원본 탭을 사용하여 선택과목 동시수업 누락을 막습니다.
  const rows=teacherTimetableMasterRows(teacher);
  if(!rows.length)return null;
  const days=["월","화","수","목","금"],week=Array.from({length:7},()=>Array(5).fill(""));
  rows.forEach(row=>{
    const p=Number(row["교시"]),d=days.indexOf(String(row["요일"]||""));
    const room=String(row["교실"]||"").trim();
    if(p>=1&&p<=7&&d>=0)week[p-1][d]=`${row["과목"]||""}|${room}`;
  });
  return {week,days,teacher,source:"UEP 교사시간표마스터",reference:true,changed:0};
}
function timetableMasterWeekDates(value){
  const base=new Date(`${String(value||dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if(Number.isNaN(base.getTime()))return [];
  const day=base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate()+(day===0?-6:1-day));
  return Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(base);d.setDate(base.getDate()+i);return d;});
}

  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");

  };

  $("#referencePreview").onclick = () => {
    const imported = parseReferencePaste($("#referencePaste").value, type);
    $("#referenceImportPreview").innerHTML = imported.length
      ? `<small>${imported.length}개 행을 확인했습니다.</small>${referenceRowsMarkup(imported.slice(0, 8), type)}`

function referenceRowsMarkup(rows, type = "") {
  if (!rows.length) return '<div class="reference-detail"><div class="outing-empty">등록된 자료가 없습니다. 표 전체 편집에서 원본 표를 붙여넣으세요.</div></div>';
  if (type === "homeroom") {
    const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
    return `<div class="homeroom-grade-sections">${grades.map((grade) => {
      const gradeRows = rows.filter((row) => String(row[0]) === grade);
      return `<section class="homeroom-grade-section"><header><span>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년</span><small>${gradeRows.length}개 학급</small></header><div class="reference-table reference-homeroom">${gradeRows.map((row) => `<div><b>${escapeHtml(row[1])}반</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "-")}</span><small>부담임 ${escapeHtml(row[3] || "-")}</small></div>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
    }).join("")}</div>`;
  }
  if (type === "extension") {
    return `<div class="reference-table reference-extension">${rows.map((row) => `<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[0] || "기타")}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "")}</span><em>${escapeHtml(row[3] || "")}</em></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="reference-detail">${rows.map((row) => `<div class="reference-row reference-approval"><b>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "")}</span>${row[2] ? `<small>${escapeHtml(row[2])}</small>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
}

function parseReferencePaste(text, type) {
  const headers = {
    approval: ["업무분류", "업무 종류", "결재라인", "비고"],
    homeroom: ["학년", "반", "담임", "부담임"],
    extension: ["구역", "이름", "시설", "자리", "대표번호", "내선"],
  }[type] || [];
  const widths = { approval: 3, homeroom: 4, extension: 4 };
  return String(text || "").split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => {
    const cells = (line.includes("\t") ? line.split("\t") : line.split(/\s{2,}|,/))
      .map((cell) => String(cell || "").trim());
    while (cells.length && !cells[cells.length - 1]) cells.pop();
    return cells.slice(0, widths[type] || cells.length);
  }).filter((row) => {
    const joined = row.join(" ");
    return row.filter(Boolean).length >= 2 && !headers.some((header) => joined === header || joined.startsWith(`${header} `));
  });
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function updateConnectionControls() {
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const status = $(".connection-title .state");
  if (status && connected) {
    status.textContent = "구글시트 연결됨";
    status.classList.remove("warn", "error");
  }
  const sync = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (sync) sync.disabled = !googleConnectionStatus?.ok;
}

function updateDashboardTimetableControls() {
  const card = $(".timetable-card");
  if (!card) return;
  card.querySelectorAll(".timetable-popup-actions").forEach(node=>node.remove());
}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

"마지막 저장자료" : escapeHtml(sourceLabel)}` : escapeHtml(neisError || "학교·학년·반·주간을 선택하세요.")}</small></div><form id="drawerTimetableN

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function updateConnectionControls() {
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const status = $(".connection-title .state");
  if (status && connected) {
    status.textContent = "구글시트 연결됨";
    status.classList.remove("warn", "error");
  }
  const sync = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (sync) sync.disabled = !googleConnectionStatus?.ok;
}

function updateDashboardTimetableControls() {
  const card = $(".timetable-card");
  if (!card) return;
  card.querySelectorAll(".timetable-popup-actions").forEach(node=>node.remove());
}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());

function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

function parseStudentLine(line) {
  const trimmed = String(line || "").trim();
  if (!trimmed) return { number: "", name: "" };
  const parts = trimmed.includes("\t")
    ? trimmed.split(/\t+/)
    : trimmed.includes(",")
      ? trimmed.split(/,+/)
      : trimmed.split(/\s+/);
  return { number: (parts.shift() || "").trim(), name: parts.join(" ").trim() };
}

  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");

function upsertNoticeCache(payload,id){
  readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};readonlyCache.notices=Array.isArray(readonlyCache.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[];
  const normalized={...payload,id:String(id||payload.id||''),status:payload.status||'게시',detail:payload.content,date:payload.postDate,receipts:readonlyCache.notices.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))?.receipts||[]};
  const index=readonlyCache.notices.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(normalized.id));
  if(index>=0)readonlyCache.notices[index]={...readonlyCache.notices[index],...normalized};else readonlyCache.notices.unshift(normalized);
  if(Array.isArray(state.workNotices)&&state.workNotices.length){state.workNotices=[];save().catch(()=>{});}
  return normalized;
}
function setNoticeBusy(form,busy,label='저장 중…'){
  const button=form?.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');if(!button)return;
  if(busy){button.dataset.originalText=button.textContent;button.disabled=true;button.textContent=label;form.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');}
  else{button.disabled=false;button.textContent=button.dataset.originalText||'저장';form.removeAttribute('aria-busy');}
}

}

=== resources\app\gyomuon.js :: 구글시트 @ 1187087 ===
function uepCompareSelectionHistory(rows=[]){
  const byStudent=new Map();
  for(const row of rows){
    const key=uepSelectionStableStudentKey(row); if(!key) continue;
    if(!byStudent.has(key)) byStudent.set(key,[]);
    byStudent.get(key).push(row);
  }
  const out=[];
  for(const [studentKey,list] of byStudent){
    const semesters=[...new Set(list.map(uepSelectionSemester).filter(Boolean))];
    for(const semester of semesters){
      const semRows=list.filter(r=>uepSelectionSemester(r)===semester);
      const pre=new Set(semRows.filter(r=>/사전|상담|구글폼|google/i.test(uepSelectionRound(r)+' '+String(r['원본시트']??''))).map(uepSelectionNormalizeCourse).filter(Boolean));
      const main=new Set(semRows.filter(r=>/본신청|리로|수강신청/i.test(uepSelectionRound(r)+' '+String(r['원본시트']??''))).map(uepSelectionNormalizeCourse).filter(Boolean));
      if(!pre.size && !main.size) continue;
      const kept=[...pre].filter(x=>main.has(x));
      const removed=[...pre].filter(x=>!main.has(x));
      const added=[...main].filter(x=>!pre.has(x));
      const status=!removed.length&&!added.length?'일치':(removed.length&&added.length?'변경':(added.length?'추가':'삭제'));
      out.push({studentKey,semester,status,kept,removed,added});
    }
  }
  return out;
}

(function(){
  function annotateGoogleConnection(){
    const drawer=document.querySelector('#drawerBody');
    if(!drawer) return;
    const text=String(drawer.textContent||'');
    if(!/Google|구글시트/.test(text)) return;
    if(drawer.querySelector('[data-uep-google-nonblocking-note]')) return;
    const note=document.createElement('div');
    note.dataset.uepGoogleNonblockingNote='1';
    note.className='setup-warning';
    note.style.marginTop='10px';
    note.innerHTML='<b>UEP는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.</b><br>Google 연결은 최신 학교자료를 동기화하기 위한 연결입니다. 연결이 끊겨도 마지막 정상 동기화 자료로 화면을 사용할 수 있으며, 연결이 복구되면 최신 자료로 자동 갱신됩니다.';
    drawer.appendChild(note);
  }
  const obs=new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(annotateGoogleConnection));
  obs.observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  setTimeout(annotateGoogleConnection,350);
})();

async function bulkAutoNoticeGovernance(status){
  if(!currentUserCanManageNotices())return;
  const rows=automaticDeadlineAlerts({includePast:true,includeDismissed:true,includeUnapproved:true})
    .filter(item=>item.days>=-3&&item.days<=7&&autoNoticeGovernanceStatus(item.source,item.id)==='pending');
  if(!rows.length)return toast('검토할 자동공지 후보가 없습니다.');
  const label=status==='approved'?'승인':status==='handled'?'확인':status==='blocked'?'불가(해제)':'처리';
  if(!confirm(`현재 자동공지 후보 ${rows.length}건을 일괄 ${label} 처리할까요?`))return;
  state.autoNoticeGovernance=state.autoNoticeGovernance||{};
  const now=new Date().toISOString(),by=currentLoginTeacherName()||'관리자';
  rows.forEach(item=>{state.autoNoticeGovernance[autoNoticeGovernanceKey(item.source,item.id)]={status,note:`관리자 일괄 ${label}`,by,at:now};});
  await save();render(state.activePage||'dashboard');toast(`자동공지 ${rows.length}건을 ${label} 처리했습니다.`);
}

function connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser(item){
  if(!item)return false;
  const grade=String(item.targetGrade||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
  if(grade&&grade!=="1"&&!currentUserCanManageNotices())return false;
  const target=String(item.targetClass||"전체").trim();
  if(!target||target==="전체"||/전체|1~9|1-9/.test(target))return true;
  if(currentUserCanManageNotices())return true;
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass();
  if(!mine)return true;
  const mineNo=mine.split("-")[1];
  return target.split(/[,·\s]+/).some(v=>String(v).replace(/[^0-9]/g,"")===mineNo||String(v)===mine);
}
function directNoticeRows({activeOnly=true}={}){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const source=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[]);
  // __UEP_08160_NOTICE_LATEST_REVISION__
  const revisionTime=item=>String(item?.modifiedAt||item?.updatedAt||item?.['수정일시']||item?.postDate||item?.date||'');
  const byId=new Map();
  source.forEach((item,index)=>{
    const id=String(item?.id||item?.noticeId||item?.['공지ID']||'').trim()||('__row_'+index);
    const prev=byId.get(id);
    if(!prev||revisionTime(item)>=revisionTime(prev))byId.set(id,item);
  });
  const connected=[...byId.values()].filter(connectedNoticeVisibleToCurrentUser);
  return connected.filter(item=>{
    if(item?.status==="삭제"||item?.status==="종료"||item?.done)return false;
    if(!activeOnly)return true;
    const start=String(item.postDate||item.date||"").slice(0,10), end=String(item.endDate||"").slice(0,10);
    if(start&&start>todayKey)return false;
    if(end&&end<todayKey)return false;
    return true;
  }).sort((a,b)=>Number(Boolean(b.important))-Number(Boolean(a.important))||String(b.postDate||b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.postDate||a.date||"")));
}

function noticeExpectedCount(item){
  const target=String(item?.targetClass||"전체");

  return Math.max(1,nums.length||1);
}

function noticeCollectionState(item){
  const receipts=item?.receipts||[],classes=noticeTargetClasses(item);
  const pending=[];
  classes.forEach(c=>{
    const rows=receipts.filter(r=>String(r.classNo||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===c);
    const confirmed=!item.confirmRequired||rows.some(r=>r.confirmed);
    const submitted=!item.submitRequired||rows.some(r=>r.submitted);
    if(!confirmed||!submitted)pending.push(`${c}반`);
  });
  return {complete:pending.length===0,pending,classes};
}
