function openWorkNoticeDetail(itemId,source="connected"){
  const local=workBoardData("notice").find(x=>String(x.id)===String(itemId));
  const connected=(Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[]).find(x=>String(x.id||x.noticeId||x.key||"")===String(itemId));
  const item=connected||local;
  if(!item)return toast("학교업무 공지를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const content=item.content||item.memo||item.description||item.detail||"등록된 상세 내용이 없습니다.";
  const directConfirmRequired=item?.type==='공지'?true:Boolean(item?.confirmRequired);/* __UEP_08162_EXISTING_NOTICE_CONFIRM_GATE__ */
  const stateInfo=noticeActionState({...item,confirmRequired:directConfirmRequired}), expected=noticeExpectedCount(item), confirmCount=Number(item.confirmedCount||0), submitCount=Number(item.submittedCount||0);
  const isHead=currentUserCanManageNotices();
  const summary=isHead?`<div class="notice-progress-admin"><span><small>확인</small><b>${confirmCount}/${expected}</b></span>${item.submitRequired?`<span><small>제출</small><b>${submitCount}/${expected}</b></span>`:""}</div>`:`<div class="notice-progress-user"><span class="${stateInfo.confirmed?'done':'pending'}">${stateInfo.confirmed?'확인 완료':'미확인'}</span>${item.submitRequired?`<span class="${stateInfo.submitted?'done':'pending'}">${stateInfo.submitted?'제출 완료':'미제출'}</span>`:""}</div>`;
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="SCHOOL WORK NOTICE";$("#drawerTitle").textContent=item.title||"학교업무 공지";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="work-notice-detail"><div class="history-summary"><span><small>성격</small><b>${escapeHtml(item.noticeKind||item.type||'일반공지')}</b></span><span><small>대상</small><b>${escapeHtml([item.targetGrade&&`${item.targetGrade}학년`,item.targetClass||'전체'].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</b></span><span><small>등록</small><b>${escapeHtml(item.author||item.department||"1학년부")}</b></span><span><small>게시</small><b>${escapeHtml(item.postDate||item.date||"-")}${item.endDate?` ~ ${escapeHtml(item.endDate)}`:''}</b></span></div>${summary}<section><h4>공지 내용</h4><p>${escapeHtml(content)}</p></section>${item.deadline?`<section class="notice-deadline-box"><h4>${escapeHtml(noticeDeadlineTypeLabel(item.deadlineType))}</h4><p>${escapeHtml(item.deadline)} · ${escapeHtml(noticeDeadlineStatus(item))}</p><small>공지 일정과 별개의 업무 처리 기한입니다.</small></section>`:''}${noticeClassCollectionMarkup(item)}<div class="drawer-actions">${isHead?`<button class="btn secondary" data-notice-edit="${escapeHtml(item.id||'')}">공지 수정</button><button class="btn ${noticeCollectionState(item).complete?'primary':'secondary'}" data-notice-close="${escapeHtml(item.id||'')}">공지 내리기</button>`:''}${!isHead&&directConfirmRequired&&!stateInfo.confirmed?`<button class="btn primary" data-notice-confirm="${escapeHtml(item.id||'')}">확인했습니다</button>`:''}${!isHead&&item.submitRequired&&!stateInfo.submitted?`<button class="btn primary" data-notice-submit="${escapeHtml(item.id||'')}">제출 완료</button>`:''}</div><p class="drawer-note">확인과 제출은 서로 다른 상태이며 44_공지확인현황에 공용으로 저장됩니다.</p></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("[data-notice-edit]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openNoticeEditor(item.id));
  $("[data-notice-close]")?.addEventListener("click",()=>closePublishedNotice(item));
  $("[data-notice-confirm]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{if(await saveCurrentNoticeReceipt(item,{confirmed:true})){closeDrawer();render(state.activePage);toast("공지 확인을 기록했습니다.");}});
  $("[data-notice-submit]")?.addEventListener("click",async()=>{if(await saveCurrentNoticeReceipt(item,{confirmed:true,submitted:true})){closeDrawer();render(state.activePage);toast("제출 완료를 기록했습니다.");}});
}

async function refreshReadonlyAfterNotice({force=false}={}){
  // 확인/승인 상태는 먼저 로컬 캐시에 즉시 반영한다. 매 클릭마다 전체 시트를 다시 읽지 않는다.
  if(force){
    const sync=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();
    if(sync?.ok&&sync.data){readonlyCache=sync.data;return;}
  }
  const refreshed=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();
  if(refreshed?.ok)readonlyCache=refreshed.data;
}
async function saveCurrentNoticeReceipt(item,change){
  const mine=currentTeacherHomeroomClass(), classNo=mine?mine.split('-')[1]:'';
  const payload={noticeId:item.id,userId:currentNoticeUserId(),teacher:currentLoginTeacherName()||'사용자',grade:'1',classNo,...change};
  const result=await window.schoolBoard?.saveNoticeReceipt?.({...payload,read:true,detailViewed:true});if(!result?.ok){toast(result?.reason||'확인 상태를 저장하지 못했습니다.');return false;}item.receipts=Array.isArray(item.receipts)?item.receipts:[];const keyIndex=item.receipts.findIndex(row=>(payload.userId&&String(row.userId)===String(payload.userId))||String(row.teacher)===String(payload.teacher));const nextReceipt={...(keyIndex>=0?item.receipts[keyIndex]:{}),...payload,read:true,detailViewed:true,confirmed:Boolean(change.confirmed),submitted:Boolean(change.submitted),modifiedAt:new Date().toISOString()};if(keyIndex>=0)item.receipts[keyIndex]=nextReceipt;else item.receipts.push(nextReceipt);/* __UEP_08162_NOTICE_RECEIPT_REFRESH__ */try{await refreshReadonlyAfterNotice({force:true});}catch{}return true;
}

function openGradeNotice(id) {
  const notice = state.gradeNotices.find((item) => item.id === id);
  if (!notice) return;
  const className = "1-1";
  const checked = notice.confirmedClasses.includes(className);
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GRADE NOTICE";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = notice.title;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML =
    `<div class="program-notice"><b>${escapeHtml(notice.author)} 공지</b><p>${escapeHtml(notice.detail)}</p></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>담임 확인 현황</h4><div class="notice-class-grid">${notice.requiredClasses.map((item) => `<span class="${notice.confirmedClasses.includes(item) ? "done" : ""}"><b>${item}</b><small>${notice.confirmedClasses.includes(item) ? "확인" : "미확인"}</small></span>`).join("")}</div></div><button class="btn ${checked ? "secondary" : "primary"}" id="confirmNoticeButton">${checked ? "확인 취소" : "1-1반 담임 확인"}</button><p class="drawer-note">현재는 로컬 시범 동작입니다. 여러 담임의 실시간 확인 상태는 공용 시트 또는 서버 연결 후 공유됩니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#confirmNoticeButton").onclick = async () => {
    notice.confirmedClasses = checked
      ? notice.confirmedClasses.filter((item) => item !== className)
      : [...notice.confirmedClasses, className];
    await save();
    openGradeNotice(id);
    render("dashboard");
  };
}

function openDrawer(type) {
  const googleConnected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const googleLabel = googleConnected
    ? `정상 · 학생 ${readonlyCache.students.length}명`
    : googleConnectionError
      ? "연결 오류"
      : googleConnectionStatus?.ok
        ? "권한 확인 필요"
        : "인증 필요";
  const neisLabel = neisData?.ok
    ? `${neisData.offline ? "저장자료" : "실시간"} · ${neisData.school?.schoolName || state.settings.neis.schoolName}`
    : neisLoading
      ? "불러오는 중"
      : "연결 확인 필요";
  const mealCount = neisData?.meals?.length || 0;
  const timetableDays = neisData?.timetable?.days?.filter((day) => day.lessons?.length).length ||
